 \\[0.2em] 		 + \frac{24}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}_{\alpha{}\alpha{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}_{zzz\beta{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 + \frac{12}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}_{zzz\alpha{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}_{z\beta{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}_{\alpha{}\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}_{z\alpha{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\beta{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}_{z\alpha{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}_{\alpha{}\alpha{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}_{z\beta{}\beta{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}_{\alpha{}\alpha{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}_{z\beta{}\gamma{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{4}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\beta{}} \cdot \Phi{}_{z\alpha{}\gamma{}\gamma{}} 
 \\[0.2em] 		 - \frac{8}{5} \cdot R_{z}^{-8} \cdot \Omega{}_{\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}} \cdot \Phi{}_{z\alpha{}\beta{}\gamma{}}
 \end{array}\right] 
\end{equation}
